    def main(self):
        """0000-MAIN-CONTROL."""
        self.perform_1000_initialization()
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
            self.perform_2000_process_transactions()
        self.perform_9000_finalization()
        # STOP RUN - In Python, this is typically the natural end of the script.

        self.CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_last_activity = self.WS_CURRENT_DATE

                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
            else:
                self.WS_REF_RECORD = ref_record

                self.WS_REF_CODE = self.WS_REF_RECORD.get('code', '')
                self.WS_REF_RATE = Decimal(str(self.WS_REF_RECORD.get('rate', '0.00')))

                self.RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[self.WS_TBL_IDX - 1].rt_rate = self.WS_REF_RATE
                self.WS_TBL_IDX += 1

        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N' #MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.

    def perform_2200_process_by_type(self):
        """2200-PROCESS-BY-TYPE."""
        match self.TXN_TYPE:
            case 'D':
                self.perform_2300_process_deposit()
            case 'W':
                self.perform_2400_process_withdrawal()
            case 'T':
                self.perform_2500_process_transfer()
            case 'I':
                self.perform_2600_process_interest()
            case _:
                self.perform_2900_handle_error()

        self.perform_2380_write_audit_trail()

    def set_pep_data(self, pep_match_score: Decimal):
        """

        """
        self.WS_PEP_STATUS = 'Y'
        self.WS_PEP_SCORE = pep_match_score

    def check_adverse_media(self):
        """16116-CHECK-ADVERSE-MEDIA."""
        #    MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-NAME TO MEDIA-SEARCH-NAME
        self.MEDIA_SEARCH_NAME = self.WS_CUSTOMER_NAME
        #    CALL 'MEDIASRCH' USING MEDIA-REQUEST MEDIA-RESPONSE
        self._call_mediasrch(self.MEDIA_REQUEST, self.MEDIA_RESPONSE)
        #    IF MEDIA-HITS-FOUND > 0

            #       ADD MEDIA-HITS-FOUND TO WS-WATCHLIST-HITS
            self.WS_WATCHLIST_HITS += self.MEDIA_HITS_FOUND

    def _call_mediasrch(self, media_request: MediaRequest, media_response: MediaResponse):
        """Dummy call to MEDIASRCH"""
        pass

    def calculate_match_score(self):
        """16120-CALCULATE-MATCH-SCORE."""
        #    IF WS-OFAC-SCORE > 0

            #       ADD WS-OFAC-SCORE TO WS-MATCH-SCORE
            self.WS_MATCH_SCORE += self.WS_OFAC_SCORE
        #    END-IF
        #    IF WS-PEP-SCORE > 0

            #       ADD WS-PEP-SCORE TO WS-MATCH-SCORE
            self.WS_MATCH_SCORE += self.WS_PEP_SCORE
        #    END-IF
        #    COMPUTE WS-MATCH-SCORE =
        #       WS-MATCH-SCORE / WS-WATCHLIST-HITS.
        self.WS_MATCH_SCORE = self.WS_MATCH_SCORE / self.WS_WATCHLIST_HITS

    def determine_disposition(self):
        """16130-DETERMINE-DISPOSITION."""
        #    EVALUATE TRUE
        #       WHEN WS-MATCH-SCORE >= 90
        #          MOVE 'CONFIRMED' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
        #          MOVE 'Y' TO WS-SAR-REQUIRED
        #       WHEN WS-MATCH-SCORE >= 75
        #          MOVE 'POTENTIAL' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
        #          MOVE 'REVIEW' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
        #       WHEN WS-MATCH-SCORE >= 50
        #          MOVE 'WEAK' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
        #          MOVE 'CLEARED' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
        #       WHEN OTHER
        #          MOVE 'FALSE POSITIVE' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
        #          MOVE 'CLEARED' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
        #    END-EVALUATE.
        match True:
            case _ if self.WS_MATCH_SCORE >= 90:
                self.WS_MATCH_TYPE = 'CONFIRMED'
                self.WS_SAR_REQUIRED = 'Y'
            case _ if self.WS_MATCH_SCORE >= 75:
                self.WS_MATCH_TYPE = 'POTENTIAL'
                self.WS_CASE_STATUS = 'REVIEW'
            case _ if self.WS_MATCH_SCORE >= 50:
                self.WS_MATCH_TYPE = 'WEAK'
                self.WS_CASE_STATUS = 'CLEARED'
            case _:
                self.WS_MATCH_TYPE = 'FALSE POSITIVE'
                self.WS_CASE_STATUS = 'CLEARED'

    def kyc_verification(self):
        """16200-KYC-VERIFICATION."""
        #    PERFORM 16210-VERIFY-IDENTITY
        self.verify_identity()
        #    PERFORM 16220-VERIFY-ADDRESS
        self.verify_address()
        #    PERFORM 16230-VERIFY-DOCUMENTS
        self.verify_documents()
        #    PERFORM 16240-DETERMINE-KYC-STATUS.
        self.determine_kyc_status()

    def verify_identity(self):
        """16210-VERIFY-IDENTITY."""
        #    MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-SSN TO ID-VERIFY-SSN
        self.ID_VERIFY_SSN = self.WS_CUSTOMER_SSN
        #    MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-DOB TO ID-VERIFY-DOB
        self.ID_VERIFY_DOB = self.WS_CUSTOMER_DOB
        #    MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-NAME TO ID-VERIFY-NAME
        self.ID_VERIFY_NAME = self.WS_CUSTOMER_NAME
        #    CALL 'IDVERIFY' USING ID-REQUEST ID-RESPONSE
        self._call_idverify(self.ID_REQUEST, self.ID_RESPONSE)
        #    IF ID-VERIFIED = 'Y'

            #       MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-ID-STATUS
            self.WS_ID_STATUS = 'VERIFIED'
        #    ELSE
        else:
            #       MOVE 'FAILED' TO WS-ID-STATUS
            self.WS_ID_STATUS = 'FAILED'

    def _call_idverify(self, id_request: IDRequest, id_response: IDResponse):
        """Dummy call to IDVERIFY"""
        pass

    def verify_address(self):
        """16220-VERIFY-ADDRESS."""
        #    MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-ADDRESS TO ADDR-VERIFY-INPUT
        self.ADDR_VERIFY_INPUT = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ADDRESS
        #    CALL 'ADDRVERIFY' USING ADDR-REQUEST ADDR-RESPONSE
        self._call_addrverify(self.ADDR_REQUEST, self.ADDR_RESPONSE)
        #    IF ADDR-VERIFIED = 'Y'

            #       MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-ADDR-STATUS
            self.WS_ADDR_STATUS = 'VERIFIED'
        #    ELSE
        else:
            #       MOVE 'UNVERIFIED' TO WS-ADDR-STATUS
            self.WS_ADDR_STATUS = 'UNVERIFIED'

    def _call_addrverify(self, addr_request: AddrRequest, addr_response: AddrResponse):
        """Dummy call to ADDRVERIFY"""
        pass

    def verify_documents(self):
        """16230-VERIFY-DOCUMENTS."""
        #    IF WS-DOC-TYPE = 'PASSPORT'

            #       PERFORM 16232-VERIFY-PASSPORT
            self.verify_passport()
        #    ELSE IF WS-DOC-TYPE = 'LICENSE'
        elif self.WS_DOC_TYPE == 'LICENSE':
            #       PERFORM 16234-VERIFY-LICENSE
            self.verify_license()
        #    ELSE
        else:
            #       PERFORM 16236-VERIFY-OTHER-DOC
            self.verify_other_doc()

    def verify_passport(self):
        """16232-VERIFY-PASSPORT."""
        #    MOVE WS-PASSPORT-NUMBER TO PASSPORT-VERIFY-NUM
        self.PASSPORT_VERIFY_NUM = self.WS_PASSPORT_NUMBER
        #    MOVE WS-PASSPORT-COUNTRY TO PASSPORT-VERIFY-COUNTRY
        self.PASSPORT_VERIFY_COUNTRY = self.WS_PASSPORT_COUNTRY
        #    CALL 'PASSVERIFY' USING PASSPORT-REQ PASSPORT-RESP
        self._call_passverify(self.PASSPORT_REQ, self.PASSPORT_RESP)
        #    IF PASSPORT-VALID = 'Y'

            #       MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-DOC-STATUS
            self.WS_DOC_STATUS = 'VERIFIED'
        #    ELSE
        else:
            #       MOVE 'INVALID' TO WS-DOC-STATUS
            self.WS_DOC_STATUS = 'INVALID'

    def _call_passverify(self, passport_req: PassportReq, passport_resp: PassportResp):
        """Dummy call to PASSVERIFY"""
        pass

    def verify_license(self):
        """16234-VERIFY-LICENSE."""
        #    MOVE WS-LICENSE-NUMBER TO LICENSE-VERIFY-NUM
        self.LICENSE_VERIFY_NUM = self.WS_LICENSE_NUMBER
        #    MOVE WS-LICENSE-STATE TO LICENSE-VERIFY-STATE
        self.LICENSE_VERIFY_STATE = self.WS_LICENSE_STATE
        #    CALL 'LICVERIFY' USING LICENSE-REQ LICENSE-RESP
        self._call_licverify(self.LICENSE_REQ, self.LICENSE_RESP)
        #    IF LICENSE-VALID = 'Y'

            #       MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-DOC-STATUS
            self.WS_DOC_STATUS = 'VERIFIED'
        #    ELSE
        else:
            #       MOVE 'INVALID' TO WS-DOC-STATUS
            self.WS_DOC_STATUS = 'INVALID'

    def _call_licverify(self, license_req: LicenseReq, license_resp: LicenseResp):
        """Dummy call to LICVERIFY"""
        pass

    def verify_other_doc(self):
        """16236-VERIFY-OTHER-DOC."""
        #    MOVE 'MANUAL REVIEW' TO WS-DOC-STATUS.
        self.WS_DOC_STATUS = 'MANUAL REVIEW'

    def determine_kyc_status(self):
        """16240-DETERMINE-KYC-STATUS."""
        #    IF WS-ID-STATUS = 'VERIFIED' AND
        #       WS-ADDR-STATUS = 'VERIFIED' AND
        #       WS-DOC-STATUS = 'VERIFIED'

           self.WS_ADDR_STATUS == 'VERIFIED' and \
           self.WS_DOC_STATUS == 'VERIFIED':
            #       MOVE 'APPROVED' TO WS-KYC-STATUS
            self.WS_KYC_STATUS = 'APPROVED'
        #    ELSE
        else:
            #       MOVE 'PENDING' TO WS-KYC-STATUS
            self.WS_KYC_STATUS = 'PENDING'

    def sanctions_check(self):
        """16300-SANCTIONS-CHECK."""
        #    IF WS-SANCTIONS-HIT = 'Y'

            #       PERFORM 16310-ESCALATE-TO-COMPLIANCE
            self.escalate_to_compliance()
            #       PERFORM 16320-FREEZE-ACCOUNT
            self.freeze_account()

    def escalate_to_compliance(self):
        """16310-ESCALATE-TO-COMPLIANCE."""
        #    INITIALIZE WS-ESCALATION-RECORD
        self.WS_ESCALATION_RECORD = EscalationRecord()
        #    MOVE 'SANCTIONS HIT' TO ESC-REASON
        self.ESC_REASON = 'SANCTIONS HIT'
        #    MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-ID TO ESC-CUSTOMER
        self.ESC_CUSTOMER = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID
        #    MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO ESC-DATE
        self.ESC_DATE = _current_date()
        #    MOVE 'URGENT' TO ESC-PRIORITY
        self.ESC_PRIORITY = 'URGENT'
        #    WRITE ESCALATION-RECORD FROM WS-ESCALATION-RECORD.
        self._write_escalation_record(self.WS_ESCALATION_RECORD)

    def _write_escalation_record(self, escalation_record: EscalationRecord):
        """Dummy write to ESCALATION-RECORD"""
        pass

    def freeze_account(self):
        """16320-FREEZE-ACCOUNT."""
        #    MOVE 'F' TO WS-ACCOUNT-STATUS
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_STATUS = 'F'
        #    MOVE 'SANCTIONS FREEZE' TO WS-FREEZE-REASON
        self.WS_FREEZE_REASON = 'SANCTIONS FREEZE'
        #    REWRITE ACCOUNT-RECORD.
        self._rewrite_account_record()

    def _rewrite_account_record(self):
        """Dummy rewrite of ACCOUNT-RECORD"""
        pass

    def transaction_monitoring(self):
        """16400-TRANSACTION-MONITORING."""
        #    PERFORM 16410-CHECK-VELOCITY
        self.check_velocity()
        #    PERFORM 16420-CHECK-PATTERNS
        self.check_patterns()
        #    PERFORM 16430-CHECK-HIGH-RISK
        self.check_high_risk()
        #    PERFORM 16440-CALCULATE-RISK-SCORE.
        self.calculate_risk_score()

    def check_velocity(self):
        """16410-CHECK-VELOCITY."""
        #    IF WS-DAILY-TRANS-COUNT > WS-VELOCITY-THRESHOLD

            #       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-VELOCITY-FLAG
            self.WS_VELOCITY_FLAG = 'Y'
            #       ADD 20 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
            self.WS_FRAUD_SCORE += 20
        #    END-IF
        #    IF WS-DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT > WS-AMOUNT-THRESHOLD

            #       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-AMOUNT-FLAG
            self.WS_AMOUNT_FLAG = 'Y'
            #       ADD 20 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
            self.WS_FRAUD_SCORE += 20

    def check_patterns(self):
        """16420-CHECK-PATTERNS."""
        #    IF WS-ROUND-AMOUNT-COUNT > 5

            #       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PATTERN-FLAG
            self.WS_PATTERN_FLAG = 'Y'
            #       ADD 15 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
            self.WS_FRAUD_SCORE += 15
        #    END-IF
        #    IF WS-STRUCTURING-DETECTED = 'Y'

            #       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PATTERN-FLAG
            self.WS_PATTERN_FLAG = 'Y'
            #       ADD 30 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
            self.WS_FRAUD_SCORE += 30

    def check_high_risk(self):
        """16430-CHECK-HIGH-RISK."""
        #    IF WS-HIGH-RISK-COUNTRY = 'Y'

            #       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-LOCATION-FLAG
            self.WS_LOCATION_FLAG = 'Y'
            #       ADD 25 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
            self.WS_FRAUD_SCORE += 25
        #    END-IF
        #    IF WS-NEW-DEVICE = 'Y'

            #       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-DEVICE-FLAG
            self.WS_DEVICE_FLAG = 'Y'
            #       ADD 10 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
            self.WS_FRAUD_SCORE += 10

    def calculate_risk_score(self):
        """16440-CALCULATE-RISK-SCORE."""
        #    EVALUATE TRUE
        #       WHEN WS-FRAUD-SCORE >= 80
        #          MOVE 'BLOCK' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
        #          MOVE 'Y' TO WS-MANUAL-REVIEW
        #       WHEN WS-FRAUD-SCORE >= 60
        #          MOVE 'REVIEW' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
        #          MOVE 'Y' TO WS-MANUAL-REVIEW
        #       WHEN WS-FRAUD-SCORE >= 40
        #          MOVE 'MONITOR' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
        #       WHEN OTHER
        #          MOVE 'APPROVE' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
        #    END-EVALUATE.
        match True:
            case _ if self.WS_FRAUD_SCORE >= 80:
                self.WS_FRAUD_DECISION = 'BLOCK'
                self.WS_MANUAL_REVIEW = 'Y'
            case _ if self.WS_FRAUD_SCORE >= 60:
                self.WS_FRAUD_DECISION = 'REVIEW'
                self.WS_MANUAL_REVIEW = 'Y'
            case _ if self.WS_FRAUD_SCORE >= 40:
                self.WS_FRAUD_DECISION = 'MONITOR'
            case _:
                self.WS_FRAUD_DECISION = 'APPROVE'

    def suspicious_activity_report(self):
        """16500-SUSPICIOUS-ACTIVITY-REPORT."""
        #    IF WS-SAR-REQUIRED = 'Y'

            #       PERFORM 16510-GATHER-SAR-DATA
            self.gather_sar_data()
            #       PERFORM 16520-GENERATE-SAR
            self.generate_sar()
            #       PERFORM 16530-FILE-SAR
            self.file_sar()

    def gather_sar_data(self):
        """16510-GATHER-SAR-DATA."""
        #    MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-NAME TO SAR-SUBJECT-NAME
        self.SAR_SUBJECT_NAME = self.WS_CUSTOMER_NAME
        #    MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-ADDRESS TO SAR-SUBJECT-ADDR
        self.SAR_SUBJECT_ADDR = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ADDRESS
        #    MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-SSN TO SAR-SUBJECT-SSN
        self.SAR_SUBJECT_SSN = self.WS_CUSTOMER_SSN
        #    MOVE WS-TRANSACTION-AMOUNT TO SAR-AMOUNT
        self.SAR_AMOUNT = self.WS_TRANSACTION_AMOUNT
        #    MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO SAR-ACTIVITY-DATE.
        self.SAR_ACTIVITY_DATE = _current_date()

    def generate_sar(self):
        """16520-GENERATE-SAR."""
        #    INITIALIZE WS-SAR-RECORD
        self.WS_SAR_RECORD = SarRecord()
        #    MOVE SAR-SUBJECT-NAME TO SAR-REC-NAME
        self.SAR_REC_NAME = self.SAR_SUBJECT_NAME
        #    MOVE SAR-SUBJECT-ADDR TO SAR-REC-ADDR
        self.SAR_REC_ADDR = self.SAR_SUBJECT_ADDR
        #    MOVE SAR-AMOUNT TO SAR-REC-AMOUNT

        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD: WsDeclineRecord = WsDeclineRecord()
        self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD: WsCaptureRecord = WsCaptureRecord()
        self.WS_FUNDING_RECORD: WsFundingRecord = WsFundingRecord()
        self.WS_SETTLE_HEADER: WsSettleHeader = WsSettleHeader()
        self.WS_SETTLE_DETAIL: WsSettleDetail = WsSettleDetail()
        self.WS_SETTLE_TRAILER: WsSettleTrailer = WsSettleTrailer()
        self.WS_CHARGEBACK_RECORD: WsChargebackRecord = WsChargebackRecord()
        self.WS_FILE_ERROR_LOG: WsFileErrorLog = WsFileErrorLog()
        self.WS_AUTH_REC = None
        self.WS_CARD_ACCOUNT_REC = None
        self.WS_CAPTURE_REC = None
        self.WS_ORIGINAL_AUTH = None
        self.AUTH_RECORD = None

        self.SETTLEMENT_RECORD = None
        self.CHARGEBACK_RECORD = None
        self.AUTH_FILE = []
        self.CAPTURE_FILE = []

    def validate_stop_request(self):
        """29100-VALIDATE-STOP-REQUEST."""
        self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'Y'

            self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK NUMBER REQUIRED'

            self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK ALREADY CLEARED'

    def create_stop_order(self):
        """29200-CREATE-STOP-ORDER."""
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD = WsStopRecord()
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_account = self.ACCT_ID
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_check_number = self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_amount = self.WS_CHECK_AMOUNT
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_payee = self.WS_PAYEE_NAME
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_effective_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_expiry_date = self.integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 180
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_status = 'A'
        self.write_stop_record(self.WS_STOP_RECORD)

    def apply_stop_fee(self):
        """29300-APPLY-STOP-FEE."""
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_STOP_PAYMENT_FEE
        self.update_account()
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'STOP-PAYMENT'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f'Stop payment placed on check #{self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER}'
        self.send_notification()

    def safe_deposit_box(self):
        """30000-SAFE-DEPOSIT-BOX."""
        self.box_rental()
        self.box_access()
        self.box_drilling()
        self.box_billing()

    def box_rental(self):
        """30100-BOX-RENTAL."""

            self.check_availability()

                self.assign_box()
                self.create_rental_agreement()

    def check_availability(self):
        """30110-CHECK-AVAILABILITY."""
        self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'N'
        self.WS_BOX_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_BOX_IDX <= self.WS_TOTAL_BOXES:

                    self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'Y'
                    self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX = self.WS_BOX_IDX
                    break
            self.WS_BOX_IDX += 1

    def assign_box(self):
        """30120-ASSIGN-BOX."""
        self.BOX_STATUS[self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX] = 'R'
        self.BOX_RENTER[self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX] = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID
        self.BOX_RENTAL_DATE[self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX] = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE

    def create_rental_agreement(self):
        """30130-CREATE-RENTAL-AGREEMENT."""
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT = WsRentalAgreement()
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_box_number = str(self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX)
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_customer = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_start_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_annual_fee = self.WS_BOX_SIZE_FEE[self.WS_REQUESTED_SIZE]
        self.write_rental_record(self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT)

    def box_access(self):
        """30200-BOX-ACCESS."""

            self.verify_renter()

                self.log_access()
                self.escort_to_vault()

    def verify_renter(self):
        """30210-VERIFY-RENTER."""
        self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'N'

                    self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'Y'

    def log_access(self):
        """30220-LOG-ACCESS."""
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG = WsAccessLog()
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_box_number = str(self.WS_BOX_NUMBER)
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_customer = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_time = self.current_time()
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_type = 'ENTRY'
        self.write_access_log_record(self.WS_ACCESS_LOG)

    def escort_to_vault(self):
        """30230-ESCORT-TO-VAULT."""
        self.WS_DISPLAY_MSG = 'VAULT ACCESS GRANTED'
        print(self.WS_DISPLAY_MSG)

    def box_drilling(self):
        """30300-BOX-DRILLING."""

            self.validate_drilling_auth()

                self.schedule_drilling()
                self.notify_renter()

    def validate_drilling_auth(self):
        """30310-VALIDATE-DRILLING-AUTH."""
        self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'N'

            self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'

            self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'

                self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'

    def schedule_drilling(self):
        """30320-SCHEDULE-DRILLING."""
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD = WsDrillingRecord()
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD.drill_box_number = str(self.WS_BOX_NUMBER)
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD.drill_reason = self.WS_DRILLING_REASON
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD.drill_scheduled_date = self.integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 30
        self.write_drilling_record(self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD)

    def notify_renter(self):
        """30330-NOTIFY-RENTER."""
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'BOX-DRILLING'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'MAIL'
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Important notice regarding your safe deposit box'
        self.send_notification()

    def box_billing(self):
        """30400-BOX-BILLING."""
        self.WS_BOX_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_BOX_IDX <= self.WS_TOTAL_BOXES:

                    self.charge_annual_fee()
            self.WS_BOX_IDX += 1

    def charge_annual_fee(self):
        """30410-CHARGE-ANNUAL-FEE."""
        self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID = self.BOX_RENTER[self.WS_BOX_IDX]
        self.WS_FEE_AMOUNT = self.BOX_ANNUAL_FEE[self.WS_BOX_IDX]
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_FEE_AMOUNT
        self.update_account()
        self.BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL[self.WS_BOX_IDX] = self.BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL[self.WS_BOX_IDX] + 10000

    def merchant_services(self):
        """31000-MERCHANT-SERVICES."""
        self.process_authorization()
        self.capture_transaction()
        self.process_settlement()
        self.handle_chargeback()

    def process_authorization(self):
        """31100-PROCESS-AUTHORIZATION."""
        self.validate_card()

            self.check_fraud_score()

                self.check_available_credit()

                    self.approve_auth()
                else:
                    self.decline_auth()
            else:
                self.decline_auth()
        else:
            self.decline_auth()

    def validate_card(self):
        """31110-VALIDATE-CARD."""
        self.WS_CARD_VALID = 'N'
        self.check_luhn()

            self.check_expiry()

                self.check_cvv()

                    self.WS_CARD_VALID = 'Y'

    def check_luhn(self):
        """31115-CHECK-LUHN."""
        self.WS_LUHN_SUM = 0
        self.WS_LUHN_IDX = 16
        while self.WS_LUHN_IDX >= 1:
            self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT = int(self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER[self.WS_LUHN_IDX - 1])

                self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT *= 2

                    self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT -= 9
            self.WS_LUHN_SUM += self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT
            self.WS_LUHN_IDX -= 1

            self.WS_LUHN_VALID = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_LUHN_VALID = 'N'

    def check_expiry(self):
        """31116-CHECK-EXPIRY."""

            self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'N'

    def check_cvv(self):
        """31117-CHECK-CVV."""
        self.cvvverify(self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER, self.WS_AUTH_CVV, self.WS_CVV_RESULT)

            self.WS_CVV_VALID = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_CVV_VALID = 'N'

    def check_fraud_score(self):
        """31120-CHECK-FRAUD-SCORE."""
        self.fraudcheck(self.WS_AUTH_REQUEST, self.WS_FRAUD_RESPONSE)

            self.WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'N'
            self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE = self.FRAUD_DECLINE_CODE

    def check_available_credit(self):
        """31130-CHECK-AVAILABLE-CREDIT."""
        self.WS_SEARCH_KEY = self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
        self.WS_CARD_ACCOUNT_REC = self.read_card_account_file(self.WS_SEARCH_KEY)

            self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'N'
            self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE = '51'

    def approve_auth(self):
        """31140-APPROVE-AUTH."""
        self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_CODE = '00'
        self.generate_auth_code()
        self.WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT -= self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
        self.record_authorization()

    def generate_auth_code(self):
        """31145-GENERATE-AUTH-CODE."""
        self.WS_AUTH_CODE = int(self.random() * 999999)
        self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE = str(self.WS_AUTH_CODE)

    def record_authorization(self):
        """31146-RECORD-AUTHORIZATION."""
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD = WsAuthRecord()
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_card = self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_amount = self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_code = self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_time = self.current_time()
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_merchant = self.WS_MERCHANT_ID
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_status = 'P'
        self.write_auth_record(self.WS_AUTH_RECORD)

    def decline_auth(self):
        """31150-DECLINE-AUTH."""
        self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_CODE = self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD = WsDeclineRecord()
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_rec_card = self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_rec_amount = self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_rec_code = self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_rec_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.write_decline_record(self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD)

    def capture_transaction(self):
        """31200-CAPTURE-TRANSACTION."""

            self.validate_auth_code()

                self.create_capture_record()

    def validate_auth_code(self):
        """31210-VALIDATE-AUTH-CODE."""
        self.WS_AUTH_VALID = 'N'
        self.AUTH_SEARCH_KEY = self.WS_CAPTURE_AUTH_CODE
        self.WS_AUTH_REC = next((record for record in self.AUTH_FILE if record.auth_rec_code == self.AUTH_SEARCH_KEY), None)

            self.WS_AUTH_VALID = 'N'
        else:

                self.WS_AUTH_VALID = 'Y'

    def create_capture_record(self):
        """31220-CREATE-CAPTURE-RECORD."""
        self.WS_AUTH_REC.auth_rec_status = 'C'
        self.rewrite_auth_record(self.WS_AUTH_REC)
        self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD = WsCaptureRecord()
        self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD.capture_card = self.WS_AUTH_REC.auth_rec_card
        self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD.capture_amount = self.WS_CAPTURE_AMOUNT
        self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD.capture_auth_code = self.WS_CAPTURE_AUTH_CODE
        self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD.capture_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.write_capture_record(self.WS_CAPTURE_RECORD)

    def process_settlement(self):
        """31300-PROCESS-SETTLEMENT."""
        self.batch_transactions()
        self.calculate_fees()
        self.create_funding_record()
        self.send_settlement_file()

    def batch_transactions(self):
        """31310-BATCH-TRANSACTIONS."""
        self.WS_BATCH_TOTAL = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_BATCH_COUNT = 0
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
        while self.WS_EOF_FLAG == 'N':
            try:
                self.WS_CAPTURE_REC = next(record for record in self.CAPTURE_FILE if not getattr(record, 'CAPTURE_SETTLED', 'N') == 'Y')

                    self.WS_BATCH_TOTAL += self.WS_CAPTURE_REC.capture_amount
                    self.WS_BATCH_COUNT += 1
                    setattr(self.WS_CAPTURE_REC, 'CAPTURE_SETTLED', 'Y')
                    self.rewrite_capture_record(self.WS_CAPTURE_REC)
                else:
                    self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
            except StopIteration:
                self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        self.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

    def calculate_fees(self):
        """31320-CALCULATE-FEES."""
        self.WS_INTERCHANGE_FEE = self.WS_BATCH_TOTAL * Decimal("0.0175")
        self.WS_ASSESSMENT_FEE = self.WS_BATCH_TOTAL * Decimal("0.0015")
        self.WS_PROCESSOR_FEE = Decimal(self.WS_BATCH_COUNT * 0.10)
        self.WS_TOTAL_FEES = self.WS_INTERCHANGE_FEE + self.WS_ASSESSMENT_FEE + self.WS_PROCESSOR_FEE

    def update_cfp_document(self):
        """33330-UPDATE-CFP-DOCUMENT."""

    def capital_management(self):
        """34000-CAPITAL-MANAGEMENT."""
        self.calculate_capital_ratios()
        self.risk_weighted_assets()
        self.capital_planning()
        self.stress_testing()

    def calculate_capital_ratios(self):
        """34100-CALCULATE-CAPITAL-RATIOS."""
        self.calculate_tier1()
        self.calculate_tier2()
        self.calculate_ratios()

    def calculate_tier1(self):
        """34110-CALCULATE-TIER1."""
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += self.WS_COMMON_STOCK
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += self.WS_RETAINED_EARNINGS
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += self.WS_AOCI
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= self.WS_GOODWILL
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= self.WS_INTANGIBLES
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= self.WS_DTA_DEDUCTION

    def calculate_tier2(self):
        """34120-CALCULATE-TIER2."""
        self.WS_TIER2_CAPITAL = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_TIER2_CAPITAL += self.WS_SUB_DEBT
        self.WS_TIER2_CAPITAL += self.WS_ALLL_ELIGIBLE
        self.WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL = self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL + self.WS_TIER2_CAPITAL

    def calculate_ratios(self):
        """34130-CALCULATE-RATIOS."""

            self.WS_CET1_RATIO = (self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL / self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS) * 100
            self.WS_CAPITAL_RATIO = (self.WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL / self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS) * 100

            self.WS_LEVERAGE_RATIO = (self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL / self.WS_TOTAL_ASSETS) * 100

    def risk_weighted_assets(self):
        """34200-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS."""
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS = Decimal("0.00")
        self.credit_rwa()
        self.market_rwa()
        self.operational_rwa()

    def credit_rwa(self):
        """34210-CREDIT-RWA."""
        self.WS_CASH_RWA = self.WS_CASH_POSITION * Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_GOVT_RWA = self.WS_GOVT_SECURITIES * Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_BANK_RWA = self.WS_BANK_DEPOSITS * Decimal("0.20")
        self.WS_MORTGAGE_RWA = self.WS_RESIDENTIAL_MORTGAGES * Decimal("0.50")
        self.WS_COMMERCIAL_RWA = self.WS_COMMERCIAL_LOANS * Decimal("1.00")
        self.WS_CONSUMER_RWA = self.WS_CONSUMER_LOANS * Decimal("1.00")
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_CASH_RWA
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_GOVT_RWA
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_BANK_RWA
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_MORTGAGE_RWA
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_COMMERCIAL_RWA
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_CONSUMER_RWA

    def market_rwa(self):
        """34220-MARKET-RWA."""
        self.WS_MARKET_RWA = self.WS_TRADING_ASSETS * self.WS_MARKET_RISK_FACTOR
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_MARKET_RWA

    def operational_rwa(self):
        """34230-OPERATIONAL-RWA."""
        self.WS_OPERATIONAL_RWA = self.WS_GROSS_INCOME * self.WS_OPERATIONAL_FACTOR * Decimal("12.5")
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_OPERATIONAL_RWA

    def capital_planning(self):
        """34300-CAPITAL-PLANNING."""
        self.project_capital_needs()
        self.identify_capital_actions()
        self.update_capital_plan()

    def project_capital_needs(self):
        """34310-PROJECT-CAPITAL-NEEDS."""
        self.WS_PROJECTED_RWA = self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS * (1 + self.WS_GROWTH_RATE)
        self.WS_REQUIRED_CAPITAL = self.WS_PROJECTED_RWA * self.WS_TARGET_RATIO / 100
        self.WS_CAPITAL_GAP = self.WS_REQUIRED_CAPITAL - self.WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL

    def identify_capital_actions(self):
        """34320-IDENTIFY-CAPITAL-ACTIONS."""

            match True:
                case self.WS_CAPITAL_GAP <= self.WS_RETAINED_EARNINGS_PROJ:
                    self.WS_CAPITAL_ACTION = 'ORGANIC GROWTH'
                case self.WS_CAPITAL_GAP <= self.WS_SUB_DEBT_CAPACITY:
                    self.WS_CAPITAL_ACTION = 'SUB DEBT ISSUANCE'
                case _:
                    self.WS_CAPITAL_ACTION = 'EQUITY RAISE'
        else:
            self.WS_CAPITAL_ACTION = 'NO ACTION NEEDED'

    def update_capital_plan(self):
        """34330-UPDATE-CAPITAL-PLAN."""
        self.WS_PLAN_UPDATE_DATE = date.today()
        self.WS_CAPITAL_PLAN.plan_recommended_action = self.WS_CAPITAL_ACTION
        self.WS_CAPITAL_PLAN.plan_gap_amount = self.WS_CAPITAL_GAP
        # Assuming REWRITE updates the CAPITAL_PLAN_RECORD in some persistent storage
        # rewrite_capital_plan_record(self.CAPITAL_PLAN_RECORD, self.WS_CAPITAL_PLAN)
        pass # Placeholder for rewrite logic

    def stress_testing(self):
        """34400-STRESS-TESTING."""
        self.run_baseline()
        self.run_adverse()
        self.run_severely_adverse()
        self.compile_results()

    def run_baseline(self):
        """34410-RUN-BASELINE."""
        self.WS_SCENARIO_NAME = 'BASELINE'
        self.WS_RATE_SHOCK = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_GDP_CHANGE = Decimal("2.50")
        self.WS_UNEMPLOYMENT_RATE = Decimal("4.00")
        self.WS_HOUSING_DECLINE = Decimal("0.00")
        self.calculate_stress_impact()

    def run_adverse(self):
        """34420-RUN-ADVERSE."""
        self.WS_SCENARIO_NAME = 'ADVERSE'
        self.WS_RATE_SHOCK = Decimal("2.00")

def perform_4210_determine_base_premium():
    """4210-DETERMINE-BASE-PREMIUM."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT

        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = (INSURANCE_MASTER.INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT or Decimal('0.00')) / 1000 * WS_LIFE_RATE_PER_1000
    elif INSURANCE_MASTER.INS_HEALTH:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_HEALTH_BASE_PREMIUM
    elif INSURANCE_MASTER.INS_AUTO:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_AUTO_BASE_PREMIUM
    elif INSURANCE_MASTER.INS_HOME:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = (INSURANCE_MASTER.INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT or Decimal('0.00')) / 1000 * WS_HOME_RATE_PER_1000
    elif INSURANCE_MASTER.INS_UMBRELLA:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_UMBRELLA_RATE
    else:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = Decimal('0.00')

def perform_4220_apply_risk_factor():
    """4220-APPLY-RISK-FACTOR."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT

        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * Decimal('1.25')

def perform_4230_calculate_final_premium():
    """4230-CALCULATE-FINAL-PREMIUM."""
    global WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS
    INSURANCE_MASTER.INS_PREMIUM_AMOUNT = WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS += WS_CALC_AMOUNT

def perform_8500_calculate_tax():
    """8500-CALCULATE-TAX."""
    global WS_CALC_TAX

        WS_CALC_TAX = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE #COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = ...
    elif WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_2_MAX:#WHEN WS-CALC-AMOUNT <= WS-BRACKET-2-MAX
        WS_CALC_TAX = (WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + ((WS_CALC_AMOUNT - WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_2_RATE) #COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = ...
    elif WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_3_MAX: #WHEN WS-CALC-AMOUNT <= WS-BRACKET-3-MAX
        WS_CALC_TAX = (WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + ((WS_BRACKET_2_MAX - WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_2_RATE) + ((WS_CALC_AMOUNT - WS_BRACKET_2_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_3_RATE) #COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = ...
    else: #WHEN OTHER
        WS_CALC_TAX = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * WS_BRACKET_5_RATE #COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = ...

def perform_7100_analyze_patterns():
    """7100-ANALYZE-PATTERNS."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    print("ANALYZING TRANSACTION PATTERNS...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        # READ TRANSACTION-LOG NEXT
        # Simulate reading from TRANSACTION-LOG

def customer_segmentation():
    """9310-CUSTOMER-SEGMENTATION."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    print("SEGMENTING CUSTOMERS...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while WS_NOT_EOF:
        try:
            global CUSTOMER_MASTER
            customer = next(customer_master_generator)
            CUSTOMER_MASTER = customer
            calculate_clv()
            assign_segment()
        except StopIteration:
            WS_EOF = True
            WS_NOT_EOF = False

def calculate_clv():
    """9311-CALCULATE-CLV."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, CUSTOMER_MASTER
    WS_CALC_RESULT = (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_balance * WS_SAVINGS_RATE) + \
                      (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_loans * WS_PERSONAL_RATE) + \
                      (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_investments * Decimal("0.01"))

def assign_segment():
    """9312-ASSIGN-SEGMENT."""
    global WS_TEMP_CODE

        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'PLATINUM'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 5000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'GOLD'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 1000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'SILVER'
    else:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'BRONZE'

    data_quality()
    data_governance()
    metadata_management()
    data_lineage()

def etl_processing():
    """A100-ETL-PROCESSING."""
    print("RUNNING ETL PROCESSES...")
    extract_data()
    transform_data()
    load_data()

def extract_data():
    """A110-EXTRACT-DATA."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while WS_NOT_EOF:
        try:
            next(customer_master_generator)
            global WS_PROCESS_COUNT
            WS_PROCESS_COUNT += 1
        except StopIteration:
            WS_EOF = True
            WS_NOT_EOF = False

def transform_data():
    """A120-TRANSFORM-DATA."""
    cleanse_data()
    standardize_data()
    enrich_data()

def cleanse_data():
    """A121-CLEANSE-DATA."""
    global CUSTOMER_MASTER

        CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_name = "UNKNOWN"

def standardize_data():
    """A122-STANDARDIZE-DATA."""
    global CUSTOMER_MASTER
    CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_state = CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_state.upper()

def enrich_data():
    """A123-ENRICH-DATA."""
    continue_program()

def load_data():
    """A130-LOAD-DATA."""
    continue_program()

def customer_master_generator():
    """Generator for CUSTOMER-MASTER records."""
    # Replace this with actual data retrieval logic from files/databases
    # For now, let's yield some dummy data'
    for i in range(5):
        yield CustomerMaster(
            cust_total_balance=Decimal(1000 * (i + 1)),
            cust_total_loans=Decimal(500 * (i + 1)),
            cust_total_investments=Decimal(2000 * (i + 1)),
            cust_credit_score=650 + (i * 20),
            cust_last_activity=2023001 + (i * 10),
            cust_id=f"CUST{i+1}",
            cust_name=f"Customer {i+1}",
            cust_state="CA"
        )

def c100_transaction_monitoring(data: DataRecord) -> None:
    """C100-TRANSACTION-MONITORING."""
    print("MONITORING TRANSACTIONS...")
    data.WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not data.WS_EOF:
        #Simulate READ TRANSACTION-LOG NEXT

            data.WS_EOF = True
        else:
            c110_rule_based_detection(data)
            c120_behavior_analysis(data)
            c130_network_analysis(data)
            data.WS_EOF = True # force exit after 1 loop

def d110_classification(data: DataRecord) -> None:
    """D110-CLASSIFICATION."""

        data.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'A'
    elif data.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE > 650:
        data.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'B'
    elif data.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE > 550:
        data.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'C'
    else:
        data.CUST_RISK_RATING = 'D'

def d120_regression(data: DataRecord) -> None:
    """D120-REGRESSION."""
    data.WS_CALC_RESULT = (data.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE * 10) + \
                         (data.CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE / 1000) - \
                         (data.CUST_TOTAL_LOANS / 2000)

    g320_aggregator_integration(data)
    g330_marketplace_integration(data)
