def evaluate_interest_rate(ws_condition: str) -> Decimal:
    """Original COBOL: EVALUATE WS-CONDITION
    Sets the interest rate based on the provided condition.
    """
    match ws_condition:
        case "condition1":  # Example condition
            return Decimal("2.0")  # MOVE 2.0 TO WS-INTEREST-RATE
        case _:
            return Decimal("2.5")  # MOVE 2.5 TO WS-INTEREST-RATE

def calculate_simple_interest(ws_account_balance: Decimal, ws_interest_rate: Decimal, ws_days_in_period: int) -> Decimal:
    """Original COBOL: 7200-CALCULATE-SIMPLE-INTEREST.
    Calculates simple interest.
    """
    return ws_account_balance * ws_interest_rate * ws_days_in_period / Decimal("36500")
    # COMPUTE WS-SIMPLE-INTEREST = WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE * WS-INTEREST-RATE * WS-DAYS-IN-PERIOD / 36500.

def calculate_compound_interest(ws_account_balance: Decimal, ws_interest_rate: Decimal, ws_days_in_period: int) -> Decimal:
    """Original COBOL: 7300-CALCULATE-COMPOUND-INTEREST.
    Calculates compound interest.
    """
    ws_compound_factor: Decimal = (1 + ws_interest_rate / Decimal("36500")) ** ws_days_in_period
    # COMPUTE WS-COMPOUND-FACTOR = (1 + WS-INTEREST-RATE / 36500) ** WS-DAYS-IN-PERIOD
    return ws_account_balance * (ws_compound_factor - 1)
    # COMPUTE WS-COMPOUND-INTEREST = WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE * (WS-COMPOUND-FACTOR - 1).

def apply_interest(ws_interest_method: str, ws_simple_interest: Decimal, ws_compound_interest: Decimal, ws_account_balance: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """Original COBOL: 7400-APPLY-INTEREST.
    Applies calculated interest to the account balance.
    """

        ws_account_balance += ws_simple_interest
        # ADD WS-SIMPLE-INTEREST TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
    else:
        ws_account_balance += ws_compound_interest
        # ADD WS-COMPOUND-INTEREST TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
    update_account()
    return ws_account_balance
    # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT.

def fee_processing():
    """Original COBOL: 8000-FEE-PROCESSING.
    Processes fees for the account.
    """
    calculate_monthly_fee()
    # PERFORM 8100-CALCULATE-MONTHLY-FEE
    calculate_transaction_fees()
    # PERFORM 8200-CALCULATE-TRANSACTION-FEES
    apply_fee_waivers()
    # PERFORM 8300-APPLY-FEE-WAIVERS
    deduct_fees()
    # PERFORM 8400-DEDUCT-FEES.

def calculate_monthly_fee(ws_account_type: str) -> Decimal:
    """Original COBOL: 8100-CALCULATE-MONTHLY-FEE.
    Calculates the monthly fee based on the account type.
    """
    match ws_account_type:
        case 'CHK':
            return Decimal("12.00")
            # MOVE 12.00 TO WS-MONTHLY-FEE
        case 'SAV':
            return Decimal("5.00")
            # MOVE 5.00 TO WS-MONTHLY-FEE
        case 'PRM':
            return Decimal("25.00")
            # MOVE 25.00 TO WS-MONTHLY-FEE
        case _:
            return Decimal("0.00")
            # MOVE 0.00 TO WS-MONTHLY-FEE

def calculate_transaction_fees(ws_trans_count: int, ws_free_trans_limit: int, ws_per_trans_fee: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """Original COBOL: 8200-CALCULATE-TRANSACTION-FEES.
    Calculates transaction fees if the transaction count exceeds the limit.
    """

        ws_excess_trans: int = ws_trans_count - ws_free_trans_limit
        # COMPUTE WS-EXCESS-TRANS = WS-TRANS-COUNT - WS-FREE-TRANS-LIMIT
        ws_trans_fee: Decimal = ws_excess_trans * ws_per_trans_fee
        # COMPUTE WS-TRANS-FEE = WS-EXCESS-TRANS * WS-PER-TRANS-FEE
    else:
        ws_trans_fee: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
        # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TRANS-FEE
    return ws_trans_fee

def apply_fee_waivers(ws_account_balance: Decimal, ws_min_balance_waiver: Decimal, ws_customer_tier: str, ws_trans_fee: Decimal, ws_monthly_fee: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Original COBOL: 8300-APPLY-FEE-WAIVERS.
    Applies fee waivers based on account balance and customer tier.
    """

        ws_monthly_fee = Decimal("0.00")
        # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-MONTHLY-FEE

        ws_trans_fee *= Decimal("0.5")
        # COMPUTE WS-TRANS-FEE = WS-TRANS-FEE * 0.5
    return ws_trans_fee, ws_monthly_fee

def deduct_fees(ws_monthly_fee: Decimal, ws_trans_fee: Decimal, ws_account_balance: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """Original COBOL: 8400-DEDUCT-FEES.
    Deducts monthly and transaction fees from the account balance.
    """
    ws_total_fees: Decimal = ws_monthly_fee + ws_trans_fee
    # COMPUTE WS-TOTAL-FEES = WS-MONTHLY-FEE + WS-TRANS-FEE
    ws_account_balance -= ws_total_fees
    # SUBTRACT WS-TOTAL-FEES FROM WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
    update_account()
    # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT
    record_fee_transaction(ws_total_fees)
    # PERFORM 8450-RECORD-FEE-TRANSACTION.
    return ws_account_balance

def record_fee_transaction(ws_total_fees: Decimal):
    """Original COBOL: 8450-RECORD-FEE-TRANSACTION.
    Records the fee transaction.  Note:  txn_account_id and FeeRecord not defined so left as placeholders
    """
    ws_fee_record = FeeRecord()
    ws_fee_record.fee_account = txn_account_id #MOVE TXN-ACCOUNT-ID TO FEE-ACCOUNT
    ws_fee_record.fee_amount = ws_total_fees #MOVE WS-TOTAL-FEES TO FEE-AMOUNT
    ws_fee_record.fee_description = "MONTHLY FEE" #MOVE 'MONTHLY FEE' TO FEE-DESCRIPTION
    ws_fee_record.fee_date = date.today()
    write_fee_record(ws_fee_record)
    #WRITE FEE-RECORD FROM WS-FEE-RECORD.

def finalize_processing():
    """Original COBOL: 9000-FINALIZATION.
    Finalizes the processing by writing control totals, closing files, and displaying a summary.
    """
    write_control_totals()
    # PERFORM 9100-WRITE-CONTROL-TOTALS
    close_files()
    # PERFORM 9200-CLOSE-FILES
    display_summary()
    # PERFORM 9300-DISPLAY-SUMMARY.

def write_control_totals():
    """Original COBOL: 9100-WRITE-CONTROL-TOTALS.
    Writes control totals to the control record.
    """
    ws_control_record = ControlRecord()
    ws_control_record.ctl_trans_count = ws_trans_count #MOVE WS-TRANS-COUNT TO CTL-TRANS-COUNT
    ws_control_record.ctl_deposits = ws_total_deposits #MOVE WS-TOTAL-DEPOSITS TO CTL-DEPOSITS
    ws_control_record.ctl_withdrawals = ws_total_withdrawals #MOVE WS-TOTAL-WITHDRAWALS TO CTL-WITHDRAWALS
    ws_control_record.ctl_error_count = ws_error_count #MOVE WS-ERROR-COUNT TO CTL-ERROR-COUNT
    ws_control_record.ctl_run_date = date.today() #MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO CTL-RUN-DATE
    write_control_record(ws_control_record)
    #WRITE CONTROL-RECORD FROM WS-CONTROL-RECORD.

def close_files():
    """Original COBOL: 9200-CLOSE-FILES.
    Closes all files.
    """
    close_file("CUSTOMER-FILE")
    close_file("ACCOUNT-FILE")
    close_file("TRANSACTION-FILE")
    close_file("REPORT-FILE")
    close_file("ERROR-FILE")
    close_file("MASTER-FILE")
    #CLOSE CUSTOMER-FILE, ACCOUNT-FILE, TRANSACTION-FILE, REPORT-FILE, ERROR-FILE, MASTER-FILE.

def display_summary():
    """Original COBOL: 9300-DISPLAY-SUMMARY.
    Displays a summary of the processing results.  Note:  variables such as ws_trans_count are not defined at the procedure level
    so left as place holders.
    """
    print('==========================================')
    print('MEGA-ENTERPRISE PROCESSING COMPLETE')
    print('==========================================')
    print(f'TRANSACTIONS PROCESSED:  {ws_trans_count}')
    print(f'DEPOSITS:               {ws_deposit_count}')
    print(f'WITHDRAWALS:            {ws_withdrawal_count}')
    print(f'TRANSFERS:              {ws_transfer_count}')
    print(f'ERRORS:                 {ws_error_count}')
    print(f'TOTAL DEPOSITS:   $ {ws_total_deposits}')
    print(f'TOTAL WITHDRAWALS:$ {ws_total_withdrawals}')
    print(f'NET CHANGE:       $ {ws_net_change}')
    print('==========================================')

def abort_process(ws_abort_reason: str):
    """Original COBOL: 9500-ABORT-PROCESS.
    Aborts the processing and displays an error message.
    """
    print(f'CRITICAL ERROR: {ws_abort_reason}')
    print(f'PROCESSING ABORTED AT {date.today()}')
    close_files()
    # PERFORM 9200-CLOSE-FILES
    raise SystemExit(8) #STOP RUN WITH STATUS 8.

def calculate_pmi(self):
    """10335-CALCULATE-PMI."""
    match True:
        case self.ws_ltv_ratio > 95:
            self.ws_pmi_amount = self.ws_loan_amount * Decimal("0.0125") / 12
        case self.ws_ltv_ratio > 90:
            self.ws_pmi_amount = self.ws_loan_amount * Decimal("0.0100") / 12
        case self.ws_ltv_ratio > 85:
            self.ws_pmi_amount = self.ws_loan_amount * Decimal("0.0075") / 12
        case _:
            self.ws_pmi_amount = self.ws_loan_amount * Decimal("0.0050") / 12

def evaluate_history(self):
    """10340-EVALUATE-HISTORY."""

        self.ws_risk_score -= 50
        self.ws_factor_1 = 'SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY'

        self.ws_risk_score -= 30
        self.ws_factor_2 = '60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES'

        self.ws_risk_score -= 20
        self.ws_factor_3 = 'MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES'

def calculate_final_risk(self):
    """10350-CALCULATE-FINAL-RISK."""
    self.ws_risk_score = self.ws_risk_score / 4
    match True:
        case self.ws_risk_score >= 80:
            self.ws_risk_category = 'LOW RISK'
        case self.ws_risk_score >= 60:
            self.ws_risk_category = 'MODERATE'
        case self.ws_risk_score >= 40:
            self.ws_risk_category = 'ELEVATED'
        case _:
            self.ws_risk_category = 'HIGH RISK'

def determine_approval(self):
    """10400-DETERMINE-APPROVAL."""

        self.ws_approval_status = 'D'
        self.ws_conditions = 'CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW'
        return

        self.ws_approval_status = 'D'
        self.ws_conditions = 'RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED'
        return

        self.ws_approval_status = 'D'
        self.ws_conditions = 'DTI RATIO TOO HIGH'
        return
    self.ws_approval_status = 'A'
    self.calculate_approved_terms()

def calculate_approved_terms(self):
    """10450-CALCULATE-APPROVED-TERMS."""
    self.ws_approved_amount = self.ws_loan_amount
    match self.ws_credit_tier:
        case 'A':
            self.ws_approved_rate = self.ws_base_rate + Decimal("0.00")
        case 'B':
            self.ws_approved_rate = self.ws_base_rate + Decimal("0.50")
        case 'C':
            self.ws_approved_rate = self.ws_base_rate + Decimal("1.50")
        case 'D':
            self.ws_approved_rate = self.ws_base_rate + Decimal("3.00")

        self.ws_approved_rate += Decimal("0.50")

def generate_loan_terms(self):
    """10500-GENERATE-LOAN-TERMS."""
    self.ws_loan_interest_rate = self.ws_approved_rate
    self.ws_monthly_rate = self.ws_loan_interest_rate / 1200
    self.ws_compound_factor = (1 + self.ws_monthly_rate) ** self.ws_loan_term_months
    self.ws_loan_monthly_pmt = self.ws_loan_amount * self.ws_monthly_rate * self.ws_compound_factor / (self.ws_compound_factor - 1)
    self.ws_loan_principal_bal = self.ws_loan_amount

def create_amortization(self):
    """10600-CREATE-AMORTIZATION."""
    self.ws_running_balance = self.ws_loan_amount
    self.ws_payment_date = self.current_date  # Assuming current_date is available
    self.amortization_schedule: List[AmortizationEntry] = []
    for self.ws_amort_idx in range(1, self.ws_loan_term_months + 1):
        amort_entry = self.calculate_payment_split()
        self.amortization_schedule.append(amort_entry)
    return self.amortization_schedule

def calculate_payment_split(self) -> AmortizationEntry:
    """10650-CALCULATE-PAYMENT-SPLIT."""
    amort_interest = self.ws_running_balance * self.ws_monthly_rate
    amort_principal = self.ws_loan_monthly_pmt - amort_interest
    self.ws_running_balance -= amort_principal
    amort_balance = self.ws_running_balance
    amort_payment_num = self.ws_amort_idx
    amort_payment_amt = self.ws_loan_monthly_pmt

        amort_escrow = (self.ws_property_tax + self.ws_insurance_premium) / 12
        amort_total_pmt = self.ws_loan_monthly_pmt + amort_escrow + self.ws_pmi_amount
    else:
        amort_total_pmt = self.ws_loan_monthly_pmt

    self.advance_payment_date()
    amort_payment_date = (self.ws_payment_year * 10000) + (self.ws_payment_month * 100) + 1

    return AmortizationEntry(
        payment_num=amort_payment_num,
        payment_amt=amort_payment_amt,
        interest=amort_interest,
        principal=amort_principal,
        escrow=amort_escrow if self.loan_mortgage else Decimal("0.00"),
        total_pmt=amort_total_pmt,
        balance=amort_balance,
        payment_date=amort_payment_date,
    )

def advance_payment_date(self):
    """10660-ADVANCE-PAYMENT-DATE."""
    self.ws_payment_month += 1

        self.ws_payment_month = 1
        self.ws_payment_year += 1

def finalize_loan(self):
    """10700-FINALIZE-LOAN."""
    self.ws_loan_start_date = self.current_date # Assuming current_date is available
    self.ws_loan_end_date = self.ws_loan_start_date + (self.ws_loan_term_months * 30)
    self.ws_loan_status = 'A'
    self.create_loan_record()
    self.disburse_funds()
    self.send_confirmation()

def create_loan_record(self):
    """10750-CREATE-LOAN-RECORD."""
    self.ws_loan_record = {} # Assuming a dict will work
    self.ws_loan_record['LOAN-REC-ID'] = self.ws_loan_id
    self.ws_loan_record['LOAN-REC-TYPE'] = self.ws_loan_type
    self.ws_loan_record['LOAN-REC-AMOUNT'] = self.ws_loan_amount
    self.ws_loan_record['LOAN-REC-RATE'] = self.ws_loan_interest_rate
    self.ws_loan_record['LOAN-REC-PAYMENT'] = self.ws_loan_monthly_pmt
    self.ws_loan_record['LOAN-REC-START'] = self.ws_loan_start_date
    self.ws_loan_record['LOAN-REC-STATUS'] = self.ws_loan_status
    self.write_loan_record(self.ws_loan_record) # Assuming write_loan_record is defined elsewhere

def disburse_funds(self):
    """10760-DISBURSE-FUNDS."""
    self.ws_disbursement_amount = self.ws_loan_amount
    self.process_deposit() # 2300-PROCESS-DEPOSIT
    self.write_audit_trail() # 2380-WRITE-AUDIT-TRAIL

def send_confirmation(self):
    """10770-SEND-CONFIRMATION."""
    self.ws_notif_type = 'LOAN-CONFIRM'
    self.ws_notif_channel = 'EMAIL'
    self.ws_notif_subject = 'Your loan has been approved'
    self.send_notification() # 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION

def process_decline(self):
    """10800-PROCESS-DECLINE."""
    self.ws_loan_status = 'DECLINED'
    self.record_decline()
    self.send_decline_notice()

def record_decline(self):
    """10810-RECORD-DECLINE."""
    self.ws_decline_record = {}
    self.ws_decline_record['DECLINE-LOAN-ID'] = self.ws_loan_id
    self.ws_decline_record['DECLINE-STATUS'] = self.ws_approval_status
    self.ws_decline_record['DECLINE-REASON'] = self.ws_conditions
    self.ws_decline_record['DECLINE-DATE'] = self.current_date
    self.write_decline_record(self.ws_decline_record)

def send_decline_notice(self):
    """10820-SEND-DECLINE-NOTICE."""
    self.ws_notif_type = 'LOAN-DECLINE'
    self.ws_notif_channel = 'LETTER'
    self.ws_notif_subject = 'Regarding your loan application'
    self.send_notification() # 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION

def portfolio_management(self):
    """11000-PORTFOLIO-MANAGEMENT."""
    self.load_portfolio()
    self.update_market_prices()
    self.calculate_values()
    self.rebalance_check()
    self.generate_statements()

def load_portfolio(self):
    """11100-LOAD-PORTFOLIO."""
    self.ws_hold_idx = 1
    self.ws_holdings: List[Holding] = []
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while self.ws_hold_idx <= 100 and self.ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        holding_rec = self.read_holdings_file()

            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            holding = Holding(
                symbol = holding_rec['HOLD-SYMBOL'],
                shares = holding_rec['HOLD-SHARES'],
                cost_per_share = holding_rec['HOLD-COST-PER-SHARE'],
                current_price = holding_rec['HOLD-CURRENT-PRICE'],
                market_value = Decimal("0.00"),
                gain_loss = Decimal("0.00"),
                pct_change = Decimal("0.00"),
                purchase_date = 0,
                type = holding_rec['HOLD-TYPE'],
            )
            self.ws_holdings.append(holding)
            self.ws_hold_idx += 1

    self.ws_holdings_count = self.ws_hold_idx - 1

def update_market_prices(self):
    """11200-UPDATE-MARKET-PRICES."""
    for self.ws_hold_idx in range(self.ws_holdings_count):
        self.ws_quote_symbol = self.ws_holdings[self.ws_hold_idx].symbol
        self.get_quote()
        self.ws_holdings[self.ws_hold_idx].current_price = self.ws_quote_price

def get_quote(self):
    """11250-GET-QUOTE."""
    self.quote_request_symbol = self.ws_quote_symbol
    quote_response = self.getquote(self.quote_request_symbol)  # CALL 'GETQUOTE'

        self.ws_quote_price = quote_response['QUOTE-LAST-PRICE']
    else:
        self.ws_quote_price = Decimal("0.00")

def calculate_values(self):
    """11300-CALCULATE-VALUES."""
    self.ws_total_value = Decimal("0.00")
    self.ws_cost_basis = Decimal("0.00")
    self.ws_unrealized_gain = Decimal("0.00")
    for self.ws_hold_idx in range(self.ws_holdings_count):
        self.calculate_holding_value()

def calculate_holding_value(self):
    """11350-CALCULATE-HOLDING-VALUE."""
    holding = self.ws_holdings[self.ws_hold_idx]
    holding.market_value = holding.shares * holding.current_price
    self.ws_hold_cost = holding.shares * holding.cost_per_share
    holding.gain_loss = holding.market_value - self.ws_hold_cost

        holding.pct_change = (holding.gain_loss / self.ws_hold_cost) * 100
    else:
        holding.pct_change = Decimal("0.00")

    self.ws_total_value += holding.market_value
    self.ws_cost_basis += self.ws_hold_cost
    self.ws_unrealized_gain += holding.gain_loss

def rebalance_check(self):
    """11400-REBALANCE-CHECK."""
    self.calculate_current_allocation()
    self.compare_to_target()

        self.generate_rebalance_trades()

def calculate_current_allocation(self):
    """11410-CALCULATE-CURRENT-ALLOCATION."""
    self.ws_stocks_value = Decimal("0.00")
    self.ws_bonds_value = Decimal("0.00")
    self.ws_cash_value = Decimal("0.00")
    for self.ws_hold_idx in range(self.ws_holdings_count):
        holding = self.ws_holdings[self.ws_hold_idx]
        match holding.type:
            case 'STK':
                self.ws_stocks_value += holding.market_value
            case 'BND':
                self.ws_bonds_value += holding.market_value
            case 'CSH':
                self.ws_cash_value += holding.market_value

    self.ws_stocks_pct = (self.ws_stocks_value / self.ws_total_value) * 100
    self.ws_bonds_pct = (self.ws_bonds_value / self.ws_total_value) * 100
    self.ws_cash_pct = (self.ws_cash_value / self.ws_total_value) * 100

def compare_to_target(self):
    """11420-COMPARE-TO-TARGET."""
    self.ws_rebalance_needed = 'N'
    self.ws_stocks_diff = self.ws_stocks_pct - self.ws_target_stocks_pct
    self.ws_bonds_diff = self.ws_bonds_pct - self.ws_target_bonds_pct

        self.ws_rebalance_needed = 'Y'

        self.ws_rebalance_needed = 'Y'

def generate_rebalance_trades(self):
    """11430-GENERATE-REBALANCE-TRADES."""

        self.ws_sell_amount = self.ws_total_value * self.ws_stocks_diff / 100
        self.create_sell_order()
    else:
        self.ws_buy_amount = self.ws_total_value * (0 - self.ws_stocks_diff) / 100
        self.create_buy_order()

def create_sell_order(self):
    """11440-CREATE-SELL-ORDER."""
    self.ws_trade_type = 'SELL'
    self.ws_order_type = 'MARKET'
    self.ws_trade_amount = self.ws_sell_amount
    self.trade_execution() # 12000-TRADE-EXECUTION

def create_buy_order(self):
    """11450-CREATE-BUY-ORDER."""
    self.ws_trade_type = 'BUY'
    self.ws_order_type = 'MARKET'
    self.ws_trade_amount = self.ws_buy_amount
    self.trade_execution() # 12000-TRADE-EXECUTION

def monthly_statement(self):
    """11510-MONTHLY-STATEMENT."""
    self.rpt_title = 'MONTHLY INVESTMENT STATEMENT'
    self.write_holdings_detail()

def write_holdings_detail(self):
    """11515-WRITE-HOLDINGS-DETAIL."""
    for self.ws_hold_idx in range(self.ws_holdings_count):
        holding = self.ws_holdings[self.ws_hold_idx]
        self.rpt_symbol = holding.symbol
        self.rpt_shares = holding.shares
        self.rpt_price = holding.current_price
        self.rpt_value = holding.market_value
        self.rpt_gain = holding.gain_loss
        # Assuming WS-HOLDINGS-LINE is available and report_record is a method
        self.write_report_record(self.ws_holdings_line)

def quarterly_report(self):
    """11520-QUARTERLY-REPORT."""
    self.rpt_title = 'QUARTERLY PERFORMANCE REPORT'
    self.rpt_quarter_return = (self.ws_total_value - self.ws_quarter_start_value) / self.ws_quarter_start_value * 100
    self.write_report_record(self.ws_performance_line)

def annual_tax_report(self):
    """11530-ANNUAL-TAX-REPORT."""
    self.rpt_title = 'ANNUAL TAX REPORT - 1099'
    self.rpt_dividends = self.ws_dividend_income
    self.rpt_cap_gains = self.ws_realized_gain_ytd
    self.write_report_record(self.ws_tax_line)

def trade_execution(self):
    """12000-TRADE-EXECUTION."""
    self.validate_order()

        self.check_funds_shares()

            self.route_order()
            self.execute_order()
            self.settle_trade()
        else:
            self.reject_order()

def validate_order(self):
    """12100-VALIDATE-ORDER."""
    self.ws_order_valid = 'Y'

        self.ws_order_valid = 'N'
        self.ws_reject_reason = 'SYMBOL REQUIRED'
        return

        self.ws_order_valid = 'N'
        self.ws_reject_reason = 'INVALID QUANTITY'
        return

            self.ws_order_valid = 'N'
            self.ws_reject_reason = 'LIMIT PRICE REQUIRED'

def check_funds_shares(self):
    """12200-CHECK-FUNDS-SHARES."""
    self.ws_sufficient_flag = 'Y'

        self.ws_required_funds = self.ws_trade_shares * self.ws_estimated_price

            self.ws_sufficient_flag = 'N'
            self.ws_reject_reason = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'

        self.check_share_position()

            self.ws_sufficient_flag = 'N'
            self.ws_reject_reason = 'INSUFFICIENT SHARES'

def check_share_position(self):
    """12250-CHECK-SHARE-POSITION."""
    self.ws_current_shares = Decimal("0.00")
    for self.ws_hold_idx in range(self.ws_holdings_count):
        holding = self.ws_holdings[self.ws_hold_idx]

            self.ws_current_shares += holding.shares

def route_order(self):
    """12300-ROUTE-ORDER."""
    match True:
        case self.ws_trade_amount > 100000:
            self.ws_routing_type = 'ALGO'
        case self.ws_trade_amount > 10000:
            self.ws_routing_type = 'SMART'
        case _:
            self.ws_routing_type = 'DIRECT'
    self.ws_order_time = self.current_date

def execute_order(self):
    """12400-EXECUTE-ORDER."""

        self.market_order()
    elif self.order_limit:
        self.limit_order()
    elif self.order_stop:
        self.stop_order()
    else:
        self.stop_limit_order()

def market_order(self):
    """12410-MARKET-ORDER."""
    self.ws_executed_price = self.ws_current_market_price
    self.ws_trade_status = 'FILLED'
    self.ws_execution_time = self.current_date

def limit_order(self):
    """12420-LIMIT-ORDER."""

            self.ws_executed_price = self.ws_current_market_price
            self.ws_trade_status = 'FILLED'
        else:
            self.ws_trade_status = 'OPEN'
    else:

            self.ws_executed_price = self.ws_current_market_price
            self.ws_trade_status = 'FILLED'
        else:
            self.ws_trade_status = 'OPEN'

def stop_order(self):
    """12430-STOP-ORDER."""

            self.ws_executed_price = self.ws_current_market_price
            self.ws_trade_status = 'FILLED'
        else:
            self.ws_trade_status = 'OPEN'

def stop_limit_order(self):
    """12440-STOP-LIMIT-ORDER."""

        self.limit_order()
    else:
        self.ws_trade_status = 'OPEN'

def settle_trade(self):
    """12500-SETTLE-TRADE."""

        self.calculate_costs()
        self.update_positions()
        self.update_cash()
        self.record_trade()

def calculate_costs(self):
    """12510-CALCULATE-COSTS."""
    self.ws_gross_amount = self.ws_trade_shares * self.ws_executed_price
    match True:
        case self.ws_gross_amount > 100000:
            self.ws_commission = self.ws_gross_amount * Decimal("0.0005")
        case self.ws_gross_amount > 10000:
            self.ws_commission = self.ws_gross_amount * Decimal("0.001")
        case _:
            self.ws_commission = Decimal("4.95")
    self.ws_fees = self.ws_gross_amount * Decimal("0.00002")

        self.ws_net_amount = self.ws_gross_amount + self.ws_commission + self.ws_fees
    else:
        self.ws_net_amount = self.ws_gross_amount - self.ws_commission - self.ws_fees

def update_positions(self):
    """12520-UPDATE-POSITIONS."""

        self.add_to_position()
    else:
        self.reduce_position()

def add_to_position(self):
    """12525-ADD-TO-POSITION."""
    found = False
    for self.ws_hold_idx in range(self.ws_holdings_count):
        holding = self.ws_holdings[self.ws_hold_idx]

            self.ws_new_total_shares = holding.shares + self.ws_trade_shares
            self.ws_new_cost = (holding.shares * holding.cost_per_share) + (self.ws_trade_shares * self.ws_executed_price)
            holding.cost_per_share = self.ws_new_cost / self.ws_new_total_shares
            holding.shares = self.ws_new_total_shares
            found = True
            break

        self.create_new_position()

def reduce_position(self):
    """12526-REDUCE-POSITION."""
    for self.ws_hold_idx in range(self.ws_holdings_count):
        holding = self.ws_holdings[self.ws_hold_idx]

            holding.shares -= self.ws_trade_shares
            self.ws_realized_gain = self.ws_trade_shares * (self.ws_executed_price - holding.cost_per_share)
            self.ws_realized_gain_ytd += self.ws_realized_gain
            break

def create_new_position(self):
    """12527-CREATE-NEW-POSITION."""
    self.ws_holdings_count += 1
    new_holding = Holding(
        symbol = self.ws_trade_symbol,
        shares = self.ws_trade_shares,
        cost_per_share = self.ws_executed_price,
        current_price = self.ws_executed_price,
        market_value = Decimal("0.00"),
        gain_loss = Decimal("0.00"),
        pct_change = Decimal("0.00"),
        purchase_date = self.current_date,
        type = ""
    )

    self.ws_holdings.append(new_holding)

def update_cash(self):
    """12530-UPDATE-CASH."""

        self.ws_available_cash -= self.ws_net_amount
    else:
        self.ws_available_cash += self.ws_net_amount

def record_trade(self):
    """12540-RECORD-TRADE."""
    self.ws_trade_record = {}
    self.ws_trade_record['TRADE-REC-ID'] = self.ws_trade_id
    self.ws_trade_record['TRADE-REC-TYPE'] = self.ws_trade_type
    self.ws_trade_record['TRADE-REC-SYMBOL'] = self.ws_trade_symbol
    self.ws_trade_record['TRADE-REC-SHARES'] = self.ws_trade_shares
    self.ws_trade_record['TRADE-REC-PRICE'] = self.ws_executed_price
    self.ws_trade_record['TRADE-REC-COMM'] = self.ws_commission
    self.ws_trade_record['TRADE-REC-NET'] = self.ws_net_amount
    self.ws_trade_record['TRADE-REC-TIME'] = self.ws_execution_time
    self.write_trade_record(self.ws_trade_record)

def reject_order(self):
    """12600-REJECT-ORDER."""
    self.ws_trade_status = 'REJECTED'
    self.ws_reject_record = {}
    self.ws_reject_record['REJECT-ORDER-ID'] = self.ws_trade_id
    self.ws_reject_record['REJECT-REASON'] = self.ws_reject_reason
    self.ws_reject_record['REJECT-DATE'] = self.current_date
    self.write_reject_record(self.ws_reject_record)

def insurance_processing(self):
    """13000-INSURANCE-PROCESSING."""
    self.validate_policy()
    self.calculate_premium()
    self.underwriting()
    self.issue_policy()
    self.claims_handling()

def validate_policy(self):
    """13100-VALIDATE-POLICY."""
    self.ws_valid_flag = 'Y'

        self.ws_valid_flag = 'N'
        self.ws_error_msg = 'MINIMUM COVERAGE NOT MET'

        self.ws_valid_flag = 'N'
        self.ws_error_msg = 'INVALID EFFECTIVE DATE'

def calculate_premium(self):
    """13200-CALCULATE-PREMIUM."""
    match True:
        case self.policy_life:
            self.calc_life_premium()
        case self.policy_auto:
            self.calc_auto_premium()
        case self.policy_home:
            self.calc_home_premium()
        case self.policy_health:
            self.calc_health_premium()

def calc_life_premium(self):
    """13210-CALC-LIFE-PREMIUM."""
    self.ws_base_premium = self.ws_coverage_amount * Decimal("0.005")
    match True:
        case self.ws_insured_age < 30:
            self.ws_base_premium *= Decimal("0.8")
        case self.ws_insured_age < 40:
            self.ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.0")
        case self.ws_insured_age < 50:
            self.ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.5")
        case self.ws_insured_age < 60:
            self.ws_base_premium *= Decimal("2.0")
        case _:
            self.ws_base_premium *= Decimal("3.0")

        self.ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.5")

    self.ws_annual_premium = self.ws_base_premium
    self.ws_monthly_premium = self.ws_annual_premium / 12

def calc_auto_premium(self):
    """13220

def perform_insurance_calculations(ws_driver_age: int, ws_accidents_3yr: int, ws_violations_3yr: int, ws_base_premium: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """
    WHEN 6 THRU 10

    WHEN OTHER

    END-EVALUATE

    END-IF

          WS-ACCIDENTS-3YR * 200

    END-IF

          WS-VIOLATIONS-3YR * 100

    END-IF

       WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM / 12.
    """
    match ws_driver_age:
        case age if 6 <= age <= 10:
            ws_base_premium += Decimal("100.00")
        case _:
            ws_base_premium += Decimal("50.00")

        ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.5")

    ws_accident_surcharge: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

        ws_accident_surcharge = Decimal(ws_accidents_3yr * 200)
        ws_base_premium += ws_accident_surcharge

    ws_violation_surcharge: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

        ws_violation_surcharge = Decimal(ws_violations_3yr * 100)
        ws_base_premium += ws_violation_surcharge

    ws_annual_premium: Decimal = ws_base_premium
    ws_monthly_premium: Decimal = ws_annual_premium / Decimal("12.0")

    return ws_base_premium, ws_annual_premium, ws_monthly_premium

def calc_home_premium(ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_home_age: int, ws_flood_zone: str, ws_security_system: str, ws_deductible: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """
    13230-CALC-HOME-PREMIUM.

       WS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT * 0.003

       WHEN 0 THRU 10

       WHEN 11 THRU 25

       WHEN 26 THRU 50

       WHEN OTHER

    END-EVALUATE

    END-IF

    END-IF

       WS-DEDUCTIBLE / 1000 * 50

    END-IF

       WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM / 12.
    """
    ws_base_premium: Decimal = ws_coverage_amount * Decimal("0.003")

    match ws_home_age:
        case age if 0 <= age <= 10:
            ws_base_premium *= Decimal("0.9")
        case age if 11 <= age <= 25:
            ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.0")
        case age if 26 <= age <= 50:
            ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.2")
        case _:
            ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.5")

        ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.5")

        ws_base_premium *= Decimal("0.9")

    ws_deductible_credit: Decimal = (ws_deductible / Decimal("1000.00")) * Decimal("50.00")
    ws_base_premium -= ws_deductible_credit

        ws_base_premium = Decimal("200.00")

    ws_annual_premium: Decimal = ws_base_premium
    ws_monthly_premium: Decimal = ws_annual_premium / Decimal("12.0")

    return ws_base_premium, ws_annual_premium, ws_monthly_premium

def calc_health_premium(ws_insured_age: int, ws_plan_type: str, ws_family_plan: str) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal]:
    """
    13240-CALC-HEALTH-PREMIUM.

       WHEN 0 THRU 18

       WHEN 19 THRU 30

       WHEN 31 THRU 40

       WHEN 41 THRU 50

       WHEN 51 THRU 60

       WHEN OTHER

    END-EVALUATE

       WHEN 'BRONZE'

       WHEN 'SILVER'

       WHEN 'GOLD'

       WHEN 'PLATINUM'

    END-EVALUATE

    END-IF

       WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM * 12.
    """
    ws_base_premium: Decimal = Decimal("300.00")

    match ws_insured_age:
        case age if 0 <= age <= 18:
            ws_base_premium *= Decimal("0.5")
        case age if 19 <= age <= 30:
            ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.0")
        case age if 31 <= age <= 40:
            ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.3")
        case age if 41 <= age <= 50:
            ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.6")
        case age if 51 <= age <= 60:
            ws_base_premium *= Decimal("2.0")
        case _:
            ws_base_premium *= Decimal("2.8")

    match ws_plan_type:
        case 'BRONZE':
            ws_base_premium *= Decimal("0.8")
        case 'SILVER':
            ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.0")
        case 'GOLD':
            ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.3")
        case 'PLATINUM':
            ws_base_premium *= Decimal("1.6")

        ws_base_premium *= Decimal("2.5")

    ws_monthly_premium: Decimal = ws_base_premium
    ws_annual_premium: Decimal = ws_monthly_premium * Decimal("12.0")

    return ws_monthly_premium, ws_annual_premium

def underwriting(policy_life: bool, policy_auto: bool, ws_bmi: int, ws_smoker_flag: str, ws_hazardous_occupation: str, ws_driver_age: int, ws_accidents_3yr: int, ws_chronic_conditions: int, ws_recent_hospitalization: str, ws_prescription_count: int, ws_recent_claims: int, ws_address_mismatch: str, ws_doc_missing: str, ws_annual_premium: Decimal) -> tuple[int, str, Decimal, str]:
    """
    13300-UNDERWRITING.

    """
    ws_risk_points: int = evaluate_risk_factors(policy_life, policy_auto, ws_bmi, ws_smoker_flag, ws_hazardous_occupation, ws_driver_age, ws_accidents_3yr)
    ws_risk_points = check_medical_history(ws_chronic_conditions, ws_recent_hospitalization, ws_prescription_count, ws_risk_points)
    ws_fraud_flag: str = "N"
    ws_uw_status: str = ""
    ws_risk_points, ws_fraud_flag, ws_uw_status = verify_information(ws_recent_claims, ws_address_mismatch, ws_doc_missing, ws_risk_points)
    ws_uw_decision: str = ""
    ws_uw_decision, ws_annual_premium = determine_decision(ws_risk_points, ws_annual_premium)

    return ws_risk_points, ws_uw_decision, ws_annual_premium, ws_fraud_flag

def evaluate_risk_factors(policy_life: bool, policy_auto: bool, ws_bmi: int, ws_smoker_flag: str, ws_hazardous_occupation: str, ws_driver_age: int, ws_accidents_3yr: int) -> int:
    """
    13310-EVALUATE-RISK-FACTORS.

       END-IF

       END-IF

       END-IF
    END-IF

       END-IF

       END-IF
    END-IF.
    """
    ws_risk_points: int = 0

            ws_risk_points += 10

            ws_risk_points += 25

            ws_risk_points += 15

            ws_risk_points += 20

            ws_risk_points += 15
    return ws_risk_points

def check_medical_history(ws_chronic_conditions: int, ws_recent_hospitalization: str, ws_prescription_count: int, ws_risk_points: int) -> int:
    """
    13320-CHECK-MEDICAL-HISTORY.

          WS-CHRONIC-CONDITIONS * 5

    END-IF

    END-IF

    END-IF.
    """

        ws_condition_points: int = ws_chronic_conditions * 5
        ws_risk_points += ws_condition_points

        ws_risk_points += 10

        ws_risk_points += 5
    return ws_risk_points

def verify_information(ws_recent_claims: int, ws_address_mismatch: str, ws_doc_missing: str, ws_risk_points: int) -> tuple[int, str, str]:
    """
    13330-VERIFY-INFORMATION.

    """
    ws_risk_points, ws_fraud_flag = check_fraud_indicators(ws_recent_claims, ws_address_mismatch, ws_risk_points)
    ws_uw_status: str = validate_documents(ws_doc_missing)
    return ws_risk_points, ws_fraud_flag, ws_uw_status

def check_fraud_indicators(ws_recent_claims: int, ws_address_mismatch: str, ws_risk_points: int) -> tuple[int, str]:
    """
    13335-CHECK-FRAUD-INDICATORS.

    END-IF

    END-IF.
    """
    ws_fraud_flag: str = "N"

        ws_risk_points += 20
        ws_fraud_flag = 'Y'

        ws_risk_points += 10
    return ws_risk_points, ws_fraud_flag

def validate_documents(ws_doc_missing: str) -> str:
    """
    13336-VALIDATE-DOCUMENTS.

    ELSE

    END-IF.
    """

        ws_uw_status: str = 'PENDING'
    else:
        ws_uw_status = 'COMPLETE'
    return ws_uw_status

def determine_decision(ws_risk_points: int, ws_annual_premium: Decimal) -> tuple[str, Decimal]:
    """
    13340-DETERMINE-DECISION.

       WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 50

       WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 30

             WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM * 1.5
       WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 15

       WHEN OTHER

             WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM * 0.9
    END-EVALUATE.
    """
    match True:
        case _ if ws_risk_points > 50:
            ws_uw_decision: str = 'DECLINE'
        case _ if ws_risk_points > 30:
            ws_uw_decision = 'SUBSTANDARD'
            ws_annual_premium *= Decimal("1.5")
        case _ if ws_risk_points > 15:
            ws_uw_decision = 'STANDARD'
        case _:
            ws_uw_decision = 'PREFERRED'
            ws_annual_premium *= Decimal("0.9")
    return ws_uw_decision, ws_annual_premium

def issue_policy(ws_uw_decision: str, generate_policy_number, create_policy_record, set_beneficiaries, send_policy_docs, send_decline_letter) -> None:
    """
    13400-ISSUE-POLICY.

    ELSE

    END-IF.
    """

        generate_policy_number()
        create_policy_record()
        set_beneficiaries()
        send_policy_docs()
    else:
        send_decline_letter()

def generate_policy_number(ws_date_part: str, ws_policy_type: str) -> str:
    """
    13410-GENERATE-POLICY-NUMBER.

       FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999
    STRING WS-TYPE-PART DELIMITED SIZE
           WS-DATE-PART DELIMITED SIZE
           WS-RANDOM-PART DELIMITED SIZE
       INTO WS-POLICY-NUMBER.
    """
    # Assuming CURRENT-DATE and RANDOM are handled elsewhere
    ws_type_part: str = ws_policy_type
    ws_random_part: int = 0 #Placeholder for Random
    ws_policy_number: str = f"{ws_type_part}{ws_date_part}{ws_random_part}"
    return ws_policy_number

def create_policy_record(ws_policy_number: str, ws_policy_type: str, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_annual_premium: Decimal, ws_effective_date: str, ws_expiration_date: str) -> PolicyRecord:
    """
    13420-CREATE-POLICY-RECORD.
    INITIALIZE WS-POLICY-RECORD

    """
    ws_policy_record = PolicyRecord()
    ws_policy_record.POLICY_REC_NUMBER = ws_policy_number
    ws_policy_record.POLICY_REC_TYPE = ws_policy_type
    ws_policy_record.POLICY_REC_COVERAGE = ws_coverage_amount
    ws_policy_record.POLICY_REC_PREMIUM = ws_annual_premium
    ws_policy_record.POLICY_REC_EFF_DATE = ws_effective_date
    ws_policy_record.POLICY_REC_EXP_DATE = ws_expiration_date
    ws_policy_record.POLICY_REC_STATUS = 'A'
    return ws_policy_record

def set_beneficiaries(ws_policy_number: str, benef_name: list[str], benef_relation: list[str], benef_pct: list[Decimal]) -> list[BeneficiaryRecord]:
    """
    13430-SET-BENEFICIARIES.

       UNTIL WS-BENEF-IDX > 5

          INITIALIZE WS-BENEFICIARY-REC

             TO BENEF-REC-NAME

             TO BENEF-REC-RELATION

             FROM WS-BENEFICIARY-REC
       END-IF
    END-PERFORM.
    """
    beneficiary_records: list[BeneficiaryRecord] = []
    for ws_benef_idx in range(5):

            ws_beneficiary_rec = BeneficiaryRecord()
            ws_beneficiary_rec.BENEF_REC_POLICY = ws_policy_number
            ws_beneficiary_rec.BENEF_REC_NAME = benef_name[ws_benef_idx]
            ws_beneficiary_rec.BENEF_REC_RELATION = benef_relation[ws_benef_idx]
            ws_beneficiary_rec.BENEF_REC_PCT = benef_pct[ws_benef_idx]
            beneficiary_records.append(ws_beneficiary_rec)
    return beneficiary_records

def send_policy_docs(ws_policy_number: str) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
    """
    13440-SEND-POLICY-DOCS.

    STRING 'Your policy ' DELIMITED SIZE
           WS-POLICY-NUMBER DELIMITED SIZE
           ' has been issued' DELIMITED SIZE
       INTO WS-NOTIF-SUBJECT

    """
    ws_notif_type: str = 'POLICY-ISSUE'
    ws_notif_channel: str = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject: str = f"Your policy {ws_policy_number} has been issued"
    return ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject

def send_decline_letter() -> tuple[str, str, str]:
    """
    13450-SEND-DECLINE-LETTER.

       TO WS-NOTIF-SUBJECT

    """
    ws_notif_type: str = 'POLICY-DECLINE'
    ws_notif_channel: str = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject: str = 'Regarding your insurance application'
    return ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject

def claims_handling(receive_claim, validate_claim, investigate_claim, adjudicate_claim, process_payment) -> None:

    """
    receive_claim()
    validate_claim()

    process_payment()

def receive_claim(generate_claim_number, ws_claim_date: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """

    """

    ws_claim_status: str = 'RECEIVED'
    return ws_claim_number, ws_claim_status

def generate_claim_number(ws_date_part: str) -> str:
    """
    13515-GENERATE-CLAIM-NUMBER.

    STRING 'CLM' DELIMITED SIZE
           WS-DATE-PART DELIMITED SIZE
           WS-RANDOM-PART DELIMITED SIZE
       INTO WS-CLAIM-NUMBER.
    """
    ws_random_part: int = 0 #Placeholder for Random
    ws_claim_number: str = f"CLM{ws_date_part}{ws_random_part}"
    return ws_claim_number

def validate_claim(ws_policy_status: str, ws_claim_type: str, ws_covered_perils: str, ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_deductible: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """
    13520-VALIDATE-CLAIM.

    """
    ws_claim_status: str = "VALIDATED"
    ws_claim_deny_reason: str = ""
    ws_claim_status, ws_claim_deny_reason = check_policy_status(ws_policy_status)

        ws_claim_status, ws_claim_deny_reason = check_coverage(ws_claim_type, ws_covered_perils, ws_claim_status, ws_claim_deny_reason)

        ws_claim_status, ws_claim_deny_reason = check_deductible(ws_claim_amount, ws_deductible, ws_claim_status, ws_claim_deny_reason)
    return ws_claim_status, ws_claim_deny_reason

def check_policy_status(ws_policy_status: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """
    13522-CHECK-POLICY-STATUS.

    END-IF.
    """
    ws_claim_status: str = "VALIDATED"
    ws_claim_deny_reason: str = ""

        ws_claim_status = 'DENIED'
        ws_claim_deny_reason = 'POLICY NOT ACTIVE'
    return ws_claim_status, ws_claim_deny_reason

def check_coverage(ws_claim_type: str, ws_covered_perils: str, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_deny_reason: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """
    13524-CHECK-COVERAGE.

    END-IF.
    """

        ws_claim_status = 'DENIED'
        ws_claim_deny_reason = 'NOT COVERED PERIL'
    return ws_claim_status, ws_claim_deny_reason

def check_deductible(ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_deductible: Decimal, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_deny_reason: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """
    13526-CHECK-DEDUCTIBLE.

    END-IF.
    """

        ws_claim_status = 'DENIED'
        ws_claim_deny_reason = 'BELOW DEDUCTIBLE'
    return ws

def determine_next_run_date(ws_last_run_date: int, ws_schedule_frequency: str) -> int:
    """
    Original COBOL:
    FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 1
              WHEN 'WEEKLY'

                    FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 7
              WHEN 'MONTHLY'

                    FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 30
              WHEN 'QUARTERLY'

                    FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 90
              WHEN 'YEARLY'

                    FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 365
           END-EVALUATE.
    """
    match ws_schedule_frequency:
        case 'WEEKLY':
            ws_next_run_date = ws_last_run_date + 7
        case 'MONTHLY':
            ws_next_run_date = ws_last_run_date + 30
        case 'QUARTERLY':
            ws_next_run_date = ws_last_run_date + 90
        case 'YEARLY':
            ws_next_run_date = ws_last_run_date + 365
        case _:
            ws_next_run_date = ws_last_run_date + 1
    return ws_next_run_date

def collect_metrics(self):
    """
    Original COBOL:
    21100-COLLECT-METRICS.

    """
    self.collect_transaction_metrics()
    self.collect_customer_metrics()
    self.collect_performance_metrics()

def collect_transaction_metrics(self):
    """
    Original COBOL:
    21110-COLLECT-TRANSACTION-METRICS.

                 AT END

                 NOT AT END

              END-READ
           END-PERFORM

                 WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT / WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT
           END-IF

    """
    self.ws_total_trans_amount = Decimal('0')
    self.ws_total_trans_count = 0
    self.ws_avg_trans_amount = Decimal('0')
    
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            self.ws_trans_rec = self.transaction_file.read()
            self.ws_total_trans_count += 1
            self.ws_total_trans_amount += self.ws_trans_rec.trans_amount
        except EOFError:
            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            

        self.ws_avg_trans_amount = self.ws_total_trans_amount / self.ws_total_trans_count
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def collect_customer_metrics(self):
    """
    Original COBOL:
    21120-COLLECT-CUSTOMER-METRICS.

                 AT END

                 NOT AT END

                    END-IF

                    END-IF

                    END-IF
              END-READ
           END-PERFORM

    """
    self.ws_active_customers = 0
    self.ws_new_customers = 0
    self.ws_churned_customers = 0

    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            self.ws_cust_rec = self.customer_file.read()

                self.ws_active_customers += 1

                self.ws_new_customers += 1

                self.ws_churned_customers += 1
        except EOFError:
            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def collect_performance_metrics(self):
    """
    Original COBOL:
    21130-COLLECT-PERFORMANCE-METRICS.

                 AT END

                 NOT AT END

              END-READ
           END-PERFORM

                 WS-RESPONSE-TIME-TOTAL / WS-RESPONSE-COUNT
           END-IF

    """
    self.ws_response_time_total = Decimal('0')
    self.ws_response_count = 0

    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            self.ws_perf_rec = self.perf_log_file.read()
            self.ws_response_time_total += self.ws_perf_rec.perf_response_time
            self.ws_response_count += 1
        except EOFError:
            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

        self.ws_avg_response_time = self.ws_response_time_total / self.ws_response_count
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def aggregate_data(self):
    """
    Original COBOL:
    21200-AGGREGATE-DATA.

    """
    self.daily_aggregation()
    self.weekly_aggregation()
    self.monthly_aggregation()

def daily_aggregation(self):
    """
    Original COBOL:
    21210-DAILY-AGGREGATION.
           INITIALIZE WS-DAILY-SUMMARY

    """
    self.ws_daily_summary = DailySummary()
    self.ws_daily_summary.daily_date = self.ws_process_date
    self.ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_count = self.ws_total_trans_count
    self.ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_amount = self.ws_total_trans_amount
    self.ws_daily_summary.daily_deposits = self.ws_total_deposits
    self.ws_daily_summary.daily_withdrawals = self.ws_total_withdrawals
    self.daily_summary_file.write(self.ws_daily_summary)

def weekly_aggregation(self):
    """
    Original COBOL:
    21220-WEEKLY-AGGREGATION.

              INITIALIZE WS-WEEKLY-SUMMARY

           END-IF.
    """

        self.ws_weekly_summary = WeeklySummary()
        self.ws_weekly_summary.weekly_week = self.ws_week_number
        self.sum_week_data()
        self.weekly_summary_file.write(self.ws_weekly_summary)

def sum_week_data(self):
    """
    Original COBOL:
    21225-SUM-WEEK-DATA.

           END-PERFORM.
    """
    self.ws_weekly_summary.weekly_trans_count = 0
    self.ws_weekly_summary.weekly_trans_amount = Decimal('0')
    for _ in range(7):
        self.ws_weekly_summary.weekly_trans_count += self.ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_count
        self.ws_weekly_summary.weekly_trans_amount += self.ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_amount

def monthly_aggregation(self):
    """
    Original COBOL:
    21230-MONTHLY-AGGREGATION.

              INITIALIZE WS-MONTHLY-SUMMARY

           END-IF.
    """

        self.ws_monthly_summary = MonthlySummary()
        self.ws_monthly_summary.monthly_month = self.ws_curr_month
        self.ws_monthly_summary.monthly_year = self.ws_curr_year
        self.sum_month_data()
        self.monthly_summary_file.write(self.ws_monthly_summary)

def sum_month_data(self):
    """
    Original COBOL:
    21235-SUM-MONTH-DATA.

                 AT END

                 NOT AT END

                    END-IF
              END-READ
           END-PERFORM

    """
    self.ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_count = 0
    self.ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_amount = Decimal('0')
    self.ws_monthly_summary.monthly_new_accounts = 0
    self.ws_monthly_summary.monthly_closed_accounts = 0

    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            self.ws_daily_sum_rec = self.daily_summary_file.read()

                self.ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_count += self.ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count
                self.ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_amount += self.ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_amount
        except EOFError:
            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def calculate_kpi(self):
    """
    Original COBOL:
    21300-CALCULATE-KPI.

    """
    self.calc_financial_kpi()
    self.calc_operational_kpi()
    self.calc_customer_kpi()

def calc_financial_kpi(self):
    """
    Original COBOL:
    21310-CALC-FINANCIAL-KPI.

                 (WS-NET-INCOME / WS-TOTAL-ASSETS) * 100
           END-IF

                 (WS-NET-INCOME / WS-TOTAL-EQUITY) * 100
           END-IF

                 ((WS-INTEREST-INCOME - WS-INTEREST-EXPENSE) /
                  WS-EARNING-ASSETS) * 100
           END-IF.
    """

        self.ws_roa = (self.ws_net_income / self.ws_total_assets) * 100

        self.ws_roe = (self.ws_net_income / self.ws_total_equity) * 100

        self.ws_nim = ((self.ws_interest_income - self.ws_interest_expense) / self.ws_earning_assets) * 100

def calc_operational_kpi(self):
    """
    Original COBOL:
    21320-CALC-OPERATIONAL-KPI.

                 (WS-ERROR-COUNT / WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT) * 100
           END-IF

              (WS-WITHIN-SLA-COUNT / WS-TOTAL-CASES) * 100

              (WS-FCR-COUNT / WS-TOTAL-CALLS) * 100.
    """

        self.ws_error_rate = (self.ws_error_count / self.ws_total_trans_count) * 100
    self.ws_sla_compliance = (self.ws_within_sla_count / self.ws_total_cases) * 100
    self.ws_first_call_resolution = (self.ws_fcr_count / self.ws_total_calls) * 100

def calc_customer_kpi(self):
    """
    Original COBOL:
    21330-CALC-CUSTOMER-KPI.

                 (WS-CHURNED-CUSTOMERS / WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS) * 100
           END-IF

              WS-MARKETING-SPEND / WS-NEW-CUSTOMERS

              WS-AVG-REVENUE-PER-CUSTOMER * WS-AVG-CUSTOMER-TENURE.
    """

        self.ws_churn_rate = (self.ws_churned_customers / self.ws_active_customers) * 100
    self.ws_acquisition_cost = self.ws_marketing_spend / self.ws_new_customers
    self.ws_lifetime_value = self.ws_avg_revenue_per_customer * self.ws_avg_customer_tenure

    Original COBOL:

    """

    self.export_json()

def export_csv(self):
    """
    Original COBOL:
    21510-EXPORT-CSV.

              TO WS-CSV-HEADER

                 AT END

                 NOT AT END
                    STRING DAILY-DATE DELIMITED SIZE
                           ',' DELIMITED SIZE
                           DAILY-TRANS-COUNT DELIMITED SIZE
                           ',' DELIMITED SIZE
                           DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT DELIMITED SIZE
                           ',' DELIMITED SIZE
                           DAILY-DEPOSITS DELIMITED SIZE
                           ',' DELIMITED SIZE
                           DAILY-WITHDRAWALS DELIMITED SIZE
                       INTO WS-CSV-LINE

              END-READ
           END-PERFORM

    """
    #OPEN OUTPUT CSV-EXPORT-FILE
    self.ws_csv_header = 'Date,TransCount,TransAmount,Deposits,Withdrawals'
    self.csv_export_file.write(self.ws_csv_header)
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            self.ws_daily_sum_rec = self.daily_summary_file.read()
            self.ws_csv_line = (
                f"{self.ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_date},"
                f"{self.ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count},"
                f"{self.ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_amount},"
                f"{self.ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_deposits},"
                f"{self.ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_withdrawals}"
            )
            self.csv_export_file.write(self.ws_csv_line)
        except EOFError:
            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    #CLOSE CSV-EXPORT-FILE
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def export_xml(self):
    """
    Original COBOL:
    21520-EXPORT-XML.

    """
    #OPEN OUTPUT XML-EXPORT-FILE
    self.ws_xml_line = '<?xml version="1.0"?>'
    self.xml_export_file.write(self.ws_xml_line)
    self.ws_xml_line = '<DailySummaries>'
    self.xml_export_file.write(self.ws_xml_line)
    self.write_xml_records()
    self.ws_xml_line = '</DailySummaries>'
    self.xml_export_file.write(self.ws_xml_line)
    #CLOSE XML-EXPORT-FILE

def write_xml_records(self):
    """
    Original COBOL:
    21525-WRITE-XML-RECORDS.

                 AT END

                 NOT AT END

              END-READ
           END-PERFORM

    """
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            self.ws_daily_sum_rec = self.daily_summary_file.read()
            self.format_xml_record()
        except EOFError:
            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def format_xml_record(self):
    """
    Original COBOL:
    21526-FORMAT-XML-RECORD.

           STRING '<Date>' DELIMITED SIZE
                  DAILY-DATE DELIMITED SIZE
                  '</Date>' DELIMITED SIZE
              INTO WS-XML-LINE

           STRING '<TransCount>' DELIMITED SIZE
                  DAILY-TRANS-COUNT DELIMITED SIZE
                  '</TransCount>' DELIMITED SIZE
              INTO WS-XML-LINE

    """
    self.ws_xml_line = '<Summary>'
    self.xml_export_file.write(self.ws_xml_line)
    self.ws_xml_line = f'<Date>{self.ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_date}</Date>'
    self.xml_export_file.write(self.ws_xml_line)
    self.ws_xml_line = f'<TransCount>{self.ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count}</TransCount>'
    self.xml_export_file.write(self.ws_xml_line)
    self.ws_xml_line = '</Summary>'
    self.xml_export_file.write(self.ws_xml_line)

def export_json(self):
    """
    Original COBOL:
    21530-EXPORT-JSON.

    """
    #OPEN OUTPUT JSON-EXPORT-FILE
    self.ws_json_line = '{"dailySummaries":['
    self.json_export_file.write(self.ws_json_line)
    self.write_json_records()
    self.ws_json_line = ']}'
    self.json_export_file.write(self.ws_json_line)
    #CLOSE JSON-EXPORT-FILE

def write_json_records(self):
    """
    Original COBOL:
    21535-WRITE-JSON-RECORDS.

                 AT END

                 NOT AT END

              END-READ
           END-PERFORM

    """
    self.ws_first_record = 'N'
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while self.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
            self.ws_daily_sum_rec = self.daily_summary_file.read()
            self.format_json_record()
        except EOFError:
            self.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    self.ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def format_json_record(self):
    """
    Original COBOL:
    21536-FORMAT-JSON-RECORD.

           ELSE

           END-IF
           STRING WS-JSON-COMMA DELIMITED SIZE
                  '{"date":"' DELIMITED SIZE"
                  DAILY-DATE DELIMITED SIZE
                  '","transCount":' DELIMITED SIZE"
                  DAILY-TRANS-COUNT DELIMITED SIZE
                  ',"transAmount":' DELIMITED SIZE
                  DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT DELIMITED SIZE
                  '}' DELIMITED SIZE
              INTO WS-JSON-LINE

    """

        self.ws_json_comma = ','
    else:
        self.ws_json_comma = ''
        self.ws_first_record = 'Y'
    self.ws_json_line = (
        f'{self.ws_json_comma}{{"date":"{self.ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_date}",'
        f'"transCount":{self.ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count},'
        f'"transAmount":{self.ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_amount}}}'
    )
    self.json_export_file.write(self.ws_json_line)

def perform_37235_log_recon_exception() -> None:

def perform_37300_intercompany_recon() -> None:
    """COBOL: 37300-INTERCOMPANY-RECON."""
    perform_37310_load_ic_balances()
    perform_37320_match_ic_pairs()
    perform_37330_report_ic_differences()

def perform_37310_load_ic_balances() -> None:
    """COBOL: 37310-LOAD-IC-BALANCES."""
    global WS_IC_COUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG

    WS_IC_COUNT = 0
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        ic_balance = read_intercompany_file()

            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            WS_IC_COUNT += 1
            WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_COUNT - 1] = ic_balance  # Adjust index to start from 0
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def perform_37320_match_ic_pairs() -> None:
    """COBOL: 37320-MATCH-IC-PAIRS."""
    global WS_IC_IDX

    WS_IC_IDX = 1
    while WS_IC_IDX <= WS_IC_COUNT:
        perform_37325_find_ic_counterpart()
        WS_IC_IDX += 1

def perform_37325_find_ic_counterpart() -> None:
    """COBOL: 37325-FIND-IC-COUNTERPART."""
    global WS_SEARCH_FROM, WS_SEARCH_TO, WS_IC_IDX2

    WS_SEARCH_FROM = WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX - 1].IC_FROM_ENTITY
    WS_SEARCH_TO = WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX - 1].IC_TO_ENTITY
    WS_IC_IDX2 = 1

    while WS_IC_IDX2 <= WS_IC_COUNT:

                WS_IC_DIFF = WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX - 1].IC_AMOUNT + WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX2 - 1].IC_AMOUNT

                    perform_37326_log_ic_diff()
                break  # EXIT PERFORM
        WS_IC_IDX2 += 1

def perform_37326_log_ic_diff() -> None:
    """COBOL: 37326-LOG-IC-DIFF."""
    global WS_IC_DIFF_REC
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC = WS_IC_DIFF_REC()

    WS_IC_DIFF_REC.ICD_FROM = WS_SEARCH_FROM
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC.ICD_TO = WS_SEARCH_TO
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC.ICD_AMOUNT = WS_IC_DIFF
    write_ic_diff_record(WS_IC_DIFF_REC)

    """COBOL: 37330-REPORT-IC-DIFFERENCES."""
    print('INTERCOMPANY RECONCILIATION COMPLETE')

        nostro_item = read_nostro_statement_file()

            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

    WS_AUDIT_RECORD.WS_AUDIT_SESSION_ID = WS_SESSION_ID

def perform_38410_move_to_archive() -> None:
    """COBOL: 38410-MOVE-TO-ARCHIVE."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        audit_record = read_audit_file()

            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:

                write_archive_audit_record(audit_record)
                delete_audit_file()

    WS_AVG_RESPONSE = Decimal(WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIME) / Decimal(WS_TRANS_COUNT) if WS_TRANS_COUNT != 0 else Decimal("0.00")
